  clamp row, a one-off hand check: take the lower bound (`Math.max(1, ...)`) out of
  `src/ui/segmentRun.ts:79`, confirm the segmentRun test now fails, and put the line back.

#### DUP-9: Hand copies of `index.html` markup in two stories and one test fixture have drifted

- **Location**: `stories/Tabs.stories.ts:19-30` and `tests/unit/tabs.test.ts:4-19` against
  `index.html:126-162`; `stories/Dropzone.stories.ts:10-32` against `index.html:92-114`
- **Issue**: Three files write out by hand markup the page already has, and each has drifted. The Tabs
  story builds every tab as a bare labelled button (`stories/Tabs.stories.ts:22-24`); the page gives each a
  `data-tab` attribute and a `<span class="tab-icon" aria-hidden="true">` child (`index.html:128-136`; the
  icons came with #25, the story was last touched in #23). Its comment says
  `Kept in sync with the markup in index.html` (`stories/Tabs.stories.ts:9-10`), which holds for the action
  icon it sits on (`index.html:144-159`) and not for the tab row built below. The Dropzone story hard-codes
  the reset icon at `width="14" height="14"` (`stories/Dropzone.stories.ts:25`) where the app stamps
  `resetIcon(15)` (`src/ui/fileLoading.ts:169`), and has no URL row (`index.html:100-103`, hidden until
  asked for). The fixture's docstring says `The tab bar as index.html has it` (`tests/unit/tabs.test.ts:4`),
  but its Full Analysis button sits inside `.tabs` where the page has it beside (`index.html:137-138`),
  carries an id (`analysisBtn`) that occurs nowhere else in the repository, lacks the page's `tab-action`
  class, and two labels are shortened. The cost is in the stories: the Chromatic Storybook snapshots are
  taken from them, so they approve a tab bar without icons and a 14 px reset icon, neither of which the
  app draws.
- **Proposed change**: Build the copies from the page, so they cannot drift again. Stories: import
  `index.html` as text (Vite's `?raw` suffix, as `src/main.ts:2` does for the changelog), take the
  `.tab-bar` and `#dropZone` elements, set the state each story shows and append `resetIcon(15)`. Test:
  mount the page with the shared `mountHtml` and `readIndexHtml`, as `tests/unit/fileLoading.test.ts:70`
  does, then add the one deliberate non-page button (`tests/unit/tabs.test.ts:12`). Smaller alternative:
  bring the three copies up to date by hand and reword the two comments to say what is mirrored.
- **Confidence it's safe**: **Medium**. The app is untouched: nothing under `src/` imports a story, and
  stories and tests are outside the type check (`configs/tsconfig.json:6-14`). The fixture edit alone is
  High. OBSERVED: `initTabs` selects `[data-tab]` and `.tab-panel` only (`src/ui/tabs.ts:10-11`), the
  test's helpers do the same (`tests/unit/tabs.test.ts:21-31`), the page carries the four `data-tab`
  buttons and the four panels the fixture copies (`index.html:128-140`, `index.html:165-171`), and no
  assertion queries the id, the class or the button's parent. The stories are Medium because the change is
  meant to be seen (their snapshots change once and have to be accepted) and because Storybook resolving a
  `?raw` import of the page is INFERRED, not run.
- **Verification**: `npx vitest run --config configs/vitest.config.ts tests/unit/tabs.test.ts` for the
  fixture. For the stories, the Chromatic (Storybook) workflow on the pull request: expect snapshot diffs
  for the Tabs and Dropzone stories and for nothing else.

#### DUP-10: CI: the two Chromatic workflows share 48 identical lines and six workflows repeat the Node setup

- **Location**: `.github/workflows/chromatic-playwright.yml:3-8` and
  `.github/workflows/chromatic-playwright.yml:11-52` against `.github/workflows/chromatic-storybook.yml:3-8`
  and `.github/workflows/chromatic-storybook.yml:11-52`; the Node block also at
  `.github/workflows/deploy.yml:20-27`, `.github/workflows/lint.yml:22-29`,
  `.github/workflows/preview.yml:18-25` and `.github/workflows/test.yml:21-28`
- **Issue**: Lines 3-8 and 11-52 of the two Chromatic workflows are byte-identical: trigger, permissions,
  the Dependabot skip, the runner pin with its comment, checkout, two report steps, Node setup and
  `npm ci`. The last eight of those lines (the Node block) are in six workflows, each with its own
  `node-version: 20`, and there is no `.nvmrc`, `.node-version` or `engines` field, so the Node version is
  written six times and nowhere else. A change to the shared preamble has to be made by hand in two to six
  files, and nothing reports a miss.
- **Proposed change**: Smallest step first: add a version file (`.nvmrc`) and point the six setup-node steps
  at it with `node-version-file`, leaving one place to change the Node version (whatever NV-31 decides is
  then a one-line edit). Larger, if wanted: a composite action under `.github/actions` for the steps that
  follow checkout. Constraints: keep both Chromatic files and their names, since two README badges address
  them by file name (`README.md:10-11`); leave the three ffmpeg installs apart, one being pinned on purpose
  (`.github/workflows/chromatic-playwright.yml:54-62` against `.github/workflows/test.yml:30-33` and
  `.github/workflows/upload-demos.yml:24-25`); the two Playwright install lines
  (`.github/workflows/chromatic-playwright.yml:65`, `.github/workflows/test.yml:48`) have differed since #5
  introduced both and are not drift; and list a composite action's directory in `.github/dependabot.yml`,
  whose one entry names `directory: "/"` (`.github/dependabot.yml:3-4`). Optional, low value: the two
  Playwright configs differ in one line (`configs/playwright.config.ts:8`,
  `configs/playwright.chromatic.config.ts:8`) and could share a factory (a change under `configs/`, so a
  version bump).
- **Confidence it's safe**: **Medium**. OBSERVED: a diff of the two ranges prints nothing, the six Node
  blocks hash identically, and there is no version file or `engines` field. Behaviour is preserved if the
  resolved Node version and the step order stay the same. Medium because a workflow edit can only be
  confirmed by running it, and two platform behaviours could not be observed here: how setup-node reads
  `node-version-file`, and whether Dependabot's `directory: "/"` reaches actions used inside a composite
  action. A `.nvmrc` needs no version bump; an `engines` field in `package.json` would.
- **Verification**: No local command. Five of the six workflows run for the pull request's branch: compare
  each one's step list, its Set up Node log (the resolved version) and its result with its previous run.
  The deploy workflow runs only on a push to main or a manual dispatch (`.github/workflows/deploy.yml:3-7`),
  so its first run after the merge is the one to check. Afterwards
  `git grep -n "node-version" .github/workflows` should print six `node-version-file` lines and no literal.

#### DUP-11: Smaller repeats

- **Issue**: Four low-value repeats, recorded so that they are known. All are optional and none is worth a
  pull request of its own: take them when the file is being edited anyway. Each was established by
  searching for the repeated text and reading every hit.

<!-- prettier-ignore -->
| Repeat | Where | Change | Safe? |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `favicon.ico` stored twice | `public/favicon.ico` and `src/assets/favicon.ico`, the same 5,028 bytes; the page links the second (`index.html:10`) | keep the `public/` copy and link `/favicon.ico`; or keep both and say why in a comment | Medium |
| The 30 frames per second assumed when the rate is unknown | `src/lib/seekReport.ts:35`, `src/ui/seekTab.ts:113`, `src/lib/cliCommand.ts:97`, `src/ui/abPanel.ts:497` | one named constant | High |
| The placeholder dash shown for a missing value | 21 string literals in six files, for example `src/lib/format.ts:5`, `src/lib/seekReport.ts:15`, `src/lib/sizeEstimate.ts:356`, `src/ui/inspectTab.ts:98`, `src/ui/analysisTab.ts:127`, `src/ui/matrixPanel.ts:126` | one exported constant | High |
| The monospace font stack of the document's stylesheet | `src/lib/analysisDoc.ts:317`, `src/lib/analysisDoc.ts:323`, `src/lib/analysisDoc.ts:339`, `src/lib/analysisDoc.ts:359`, `src/lib/analysisDoc.ts:395`, `src/lib/analysisDoc.ts:426` | one custom property in that sheet's `:root` | High, INFERRED |

- **Proposed change**: As in the table. The `break-inside` line repeated in the same stylesheet is in DC-7, and
  the font-stack site at `src/lib/analysisDoc.ts:339` goes with DC-1's optional last step. The favicon needs a word. The page links the `src/assets` copy, and
  the `public/` copy is the one served at the site root for clients that ask for `/favicon.ico` without
  reading the page (INFERRED from how Vite treats `public/`), so keeping one file means linking the public
  one. That changes the address the page requests for the icon, not the image, which is why the row is
  Medium and why keeping both, with a one-line comment in `index.html`, is a defensible alternative. For
  the two constants, `src/lib/format.ts` is a safe home: it imports nothing, so every file in the two rows
  can import from it without closing an import cycle.
- **Confidence it's safe**: **High** for the two constants. OBSERVED: `git grep` finds 22 occurrences of the
  placeholder in `src`, one of them in a comment (`src/lib/format.ts:32`); the four fallback sites were
  read, and two are the same expression, `state.fps || 30`; a constant changes no value. **High, INFERRED**
  for the stylesheet row: the rendering is unchanged, but the text of the stylesheet inlined in every
  exported HTML file changes, and no test reads that text beyond `tests/unit/analysisDoc.test.ts:112`.
  **Medium** for the favicon. OBSERVED: equal SHA-256 for the two files. INFERRED: that the build rewrites
  a root-relative link to a `public/` file against its base, which the shared Vite configuration sets to
  `./` (line 37 of its `vite.js`), so the built page still finds the icon when it is served from a
  sub-path.
- **Verification**: `npm run typecheck && npm test` for the constants. For the stylesheet row,
  `npx vitest run --config configs/vitest.config.ts tests/unit/analysisDoc.test.ts` and a look at the Full
  Analysis preview and its print preview. For the favicon, `npm run build`, then the tab icon and the
  request for `favicon.ico` in a preview deployment.

### Consolidation

#### CON-1: State that restates other state

- **Issue**: Five pieces of shared state hold something another piece already says: two `AppState` members
  read only for a count that a neighbouring array's length already gives, one that mirrors a field of
  `state.source`, a five-member copy that works only because two interfaces spell their members alike, and a
  flag that moves in step with a module variable. Each is one more value a writer must keep in step and a
  reader must choose between, and the tests set several of them by hand beside the value they restate.
  Established by listing every writer and reader of each member with `git grep` over `src`, `tests` and
  `stories`, then reading each site.

<!-- prettier-ignore -->
| Location | What is there | What it restates | Change | Safe? |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `src/ui/fileLoading.ts:95-96` | `Object.assign(state, analysis)` copies the five members of `SampleAnalysis` (`src/lib/types.ts:76-82`) onto `AppState` (`src/lib/types.ts:297`, `src/lib/types.ts:299-302`) | nothing ties the two declarations together: the copy works because the names coincide | five explicit assignments, or let `AppState` extend `SampleAnalysis` and delete its five duplicate lines | High |
| `src/lib/types.ts:299`, `src/lib/state.ts:30` | `AppState.keyframeDecodeIndices`; both readers take only its length (`src/ui/seekTab.ts:34`, `src/ui/analysisTab.ts:280`) | `state.gopLengths.length`: one GOP is pushed per keyframe (`src/lib/mp4boxParser.ts:119-124`), and both arrays reach the state in the same copy | print the length of `state.gopLengths` at both sites; drop the member from `AppState` | Medium |
| `src/lib/types.ts:294`, `src/lib/state.ts:25`, `src/ui/fileLoading.ts:112` | `AppState.frameCount`, written once per load, read at `src/ui/inspectTab.ts:151` and `src/ui/analysisTab.ts:146` | `state.samples.length`, which `src/ui/seekTab.ts:33` and `src/ui/analysisTab.ts:279` already print as "Total Frames" | pass `state.samples.length` at the two sites; drop the member and the write | Medium |
| `src/lib/types.ts:285`, `src/lib/state.ts:16`, `src/ui/fileLoading.ts:80` | `AppState.file`, read at `src/ui/segmentRun.ts:125` and `src/ui/inBrowserEncode.ts:57` | `state.source.file`, for every source the two factories return (`src/lib/chunkedSource.ts:17`, `src/lib/chunkedSource.ts:63`; both are null for a URL read over ranges) | optional: read `state.source.file` at the two sites and drop the member | Medium |
| `src/lib/types.ts:227`, `src/lib/state.ts:51`, `src/ui/compareTab.ts:493`, `src/ui/compareTab.ts:617` | `matrix.running`, read at `src/ui/compareTab.ts:293`, `src/ui/compareTab.ts:749` and `src/ui/compareTab.ts:779` | whether `activeQueue` is non-null: the two are written on adjacent lines (`src/ui/compareTab.ts:493-494`, `src/ui/compareTab.ts:616-617`) | only once NV-5 is settled: drop the field and test `activeQueue` at the three sites | Medium |

- **Proposed change**: Work down the table. (1) Make the copy explicit, or let the type carry it. Today a
  rename of one of the five members on the `AppState` side compiles and leaves the declared field at its
  initial value after every load. By this review's reading the load's unit test would not notice for four of
  the five, because it mocks the producer with hand-written keys (`tests/unit/fileLoading.test.ts:22-28`), and
  the integration suite fails only for an emptied `samples`, or for an emptied `keyframeTimestampsSec` where
  the browser can decode H.264 (its seeking test skips itself otherwise,
  `tests/integration/smoke.spec.ts:157-179`). INFERRED: for the other three the Chromatic snapshot of a loaded
  file (`tests/chromatic/app.chromatic.test.ts:19-24`) is the one place it could show. It is also the only
  line that fills `samples`, and a search for an assignment to `state.samples` does not find it. (2) Replace
  the two counts. With `keyframeDecodeIndices` off `AppState` the copy becomes four assignments; or the member
  leaves `SampleAnalysis` as well and the array stays a local of `extractSampleAnalysis`
  (`src/lib/mp4boxParser.ts:115`), in which case the assertion at `tests/unit/mp4boxParser.test.ts:271` and
  the mock's key at `tests/unit/fileLoading.test.ts:24` go with it. `tests/unit/fileLoading.test.ts:105`
  asserts `state.frameCount` itself and would assert the sample count instead; the values set by hand at
  `tests/unit/inspectTab.test.ts:113`, `tests/unit/seekTab.test.ts:443`, `tests/unit/analysisTab.test.ts:54`,
  `tests/unit/analysisTab.test.ts:440`, `tests/unit/analysisTab.test.ts:451` (indices) and
  `tests/unit/inspectTab.test.ts:366`, `tests/unit/analysisTab.test.ts:52` (count) become dead and are
  deleted. With `frameCount` gone, the null arm of `describeFrameCount` (`src/lib/format.ts:41-43`) has no
  caller left in `src` and can go with it, together with the assertion at `tests/unit/format.test.ts:105`;
  the app does not reach that arm today either. (3) `AppState.file` is optional and the least valuable row:
  the tests set and assert the member apart from the source (`tests/unit/segmentRun.test.ts:116`,
  `tests/unit/segmentRun.test.ts:475`, `tests/unit/compareTab.test.ts:170`,
  `tests/unit/inBrowserEncode.test.ts:57`, `tests/unit/inBrowserEncode.test.ts:99`,
  `tests/unit/fileLoading.test.ts:101`), two more pass it as the expected argument
  (`tests/unit/inBrowserEncode.test.ts:95`, `tests/unit/segmentRun.test.ts:331`), and the stub source at
  `tests/unit/inBrowserEncode.test.ts:56` has no `file` of its own. (4) `matrix.running` waits for NV-5. It
  does not restate `encodeTest.running`: `runMatrix` tests that flag at `src/ui/compareTab.ts:320` and awaits
  at `src/ui/compareTab.ts:332` before the sweep raises it, so a sample run started in the gap can end and
  clear the shared flag (`src/ui/segmentRun.ts:505`) under a live sweep. What it restates is `activeQueue`, so
  once the overlap is closed: remove the field and its two writes, test `activeQueue` at the three readers,
  and delete the assertion at `tests/unit/compareTab.test.ts:872`. Do not put `encodeTest.running` in its
  place at `src/ui/compareTab.ts:293` or `src/ui/compareTab.ts:749`: in the overlap that would unlock the grid
  in the middle of a sweep.
- **Confidence it's safe**: **High** for the copy, **Medium** for the other four rows. OBSERVED for the copy:
  the producer returns exactly those five own properties (`src/lib/mp4boxParser.ts:131`) and so does its only
  mock, so explicit assignments leave the same values on `state`, and an `extends` clause is erased at build.
  A rename on the producer's side is already caught by `tests/unit/mp4boxParser.test.ts:269-282`. Keyframe
  count, OBSERVED: the two lengths are equal in every state the app writes (one writer at
  `src/ui/fileLoading.ts:96`, one reset at `src/lib/state.ts:106`), and the two tests that assert the figure
  (`tests/unit/seekTab.test.ts:449`, `tests/unit/analysisTab.test.ts:182`) set `gopLengths` to the matching
  length (`tests/unit/seekTab.test.ts:444`, `tests/unit/analysisTab.test.ts:55`). Frame count, OBSERVED: both
  readers sit behind a video-track check (`src/ui/inspectTab.ts:135-136`, `src/ui/analysisTab.ts:467`) and
  `state.tracks` is set two synchronous lines before `frameCount` (`src/ui/fileLoading.ts:110-112`), so the
  two values are equal whenever "Frames" is drawn. They differ only while two loads overlap and a re-render
  lands between them, a state in which the page already mixes two files (NV-12); the one test that asserts the figure
  sets 600 beside 600 samples (`tests/unit/inspectTab.test.ts:386`). `AppState.file`, OBSERVED: the value
  stored at `src/ui/fileLoading.ts:80` is the `File` the source holds, or null where it holds none
  (`src/ui/fileLoading.ts:72-78`). These three rows are Medium because tests hold the members by hand and
  `tests/` is outside the type check (`configs/tsconfig.json:6-14`), so a missed test edit shows only when the
  suite runs, and a value still set on a removed member not at all. `matrix.running`, INFERRED by reading:
  against `activeQueue` the flag differs between a file load or a Reset during a sweep (`resetState` clears
  the flag at `src/lib/state.ts:107`, the queue stays until the sweep's `finally`) and the end of that sweep,
  and otherwise only where two sweeps overlap (NV-5). After such a load the new file's grid is empty, so both
  early returns apply (`src/ui/compareTab.ts:743-746`, `src/ui/compareTab.ts:293`), but a second sweep started
  inside that window produces at least one transient difference, in the summary line at
  `src/ui/compareTab.ts:762`. No existing test runs two overlapping sweeps.
- **Verification**: `npm run typecheck`, then
  `npx vitest run --config configs/vitest.config.ts tests/unit/fileLoading.test.ts tests/unit/mp4boxParser.test.ts tests/unit/seekTab.test.ts tests/unit/analysisTab.test.ts tests/unit/inspectTab.test.ts tests/unit/format.test.ts`
  for the first three rows;
  `npx vitest run --config configs/vitest.config.ts tests/unit/segmentRun.test.ts tests/unit/inBrowserEncode.test.ts tests/unit/compareTab.test.ts tests/unit/encodeTab.test.ts`
  for the last two; then `npm run test:integration`.

#### CON-2: The single-start-time model survives as fallbacks behind the windows list

- **Issue**: A run was once described by one start time and one segment length (the docstring at
  `src/ui/compareTab.ts:700` still says so). It now stores a list of windows, and the older model lives on as
  fallbacks behind that list, at the thirteen sites below. Seven cannot be reached in the app, three are
  reached only when the sampler returns no windows (a file whose length is 0 or unknown, or a negative number
  typed into Duration: the unknown-length path below), two are live on purpose and one is half of each, yet
  they all read alike. They keep one state field (`segDuration`) alive for a branch nothing takes, and tests
  pin states the app never produces. Established by listing every writer and reader of `encodeTest.windows`,
  `encodedSize`, `segDuration` and `startTime` and of `matrix.windows`, `segmentStart` and `segmentLength`,
  plus the two production callers of `loadEncodedIntoAB`. Each code site is in one of three states; the last
  row is comments.

<!-- prettier-ignore -->
| Site | What it is | State | Change |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `src/lib/sizeEstimate.ts:309-316` | the one-window stand-in `currentSizeEstimate` builds from `startTime`, `segDuration` and `duration` when `encodeTest.windows` is empty | unreachable in the app: `encodedSize` and `windows` are written together, never empty (`src/ui/abPanel.ts:103-104`), and reset together | remove: pass `encodeTest.windows` straight through; the test at `tests/unit/sizeEstimate.test.ts:439-445` goes |
| `src/lib/types.ts:257-258`, `src/lib/state.ts:72`, `src/ui/abPanel.ts:100` | `encodeTest.segDuration` | unreachable in the app: its only reader is `src/lib/sizeEstimate.ts:314`, inside the row above; the reel's length is recomputed at `src/ui/abPanel.ts:160-163` | remove with the row above |
| `src/ui/compareTab.ts:736` | the third argument to `estimateForWindows` | unreachable in the app: the list passed with it is never empty | remove (DC-3) |
| `src/ui/abPanel.ts:60`, `src/ui/abPanel.ts:103`, `src/ui/abPanel.ts:104` | `totals` is optional, with a fallback for its absence at lines 103 and 104 | unreachable in the app: both callers pass it (`src/ui/compareTab.ts:667-670`, `src/ui/encodeTab.ts:273`) | removable, maintainers' call: a helper and 7 direct calls in `tests/unit/abPanel.test.ts` omit it (NV-35) |
| `src/ui/abPanel.ts:128` | `describeSampledStretches` falls back to `startTime` and `duration` | unreachable in the app: both callers run only after a load has filled the list (`src/ui/abPanel.ts:143`, `src/ui/analysisTab.ts:341-346`) | removable, maintainers' call: pinned by `tests/unit/abPanel.test.ts:751-755` |
| `src/ui/abPanel.ts:85` | a stretch's window falls back to `startTime` | unreachable in the app unless two runs overlap (NV-5): the same `??` also covers more blobs than windows | keep: without it that case is a TypeError at `src/ui/abPanel.ts:240` or `src/ui/abPanel.ts:458` |
| `src/lib/sizeEstimate.ts:39-42`, `src/lib/sizeEstimate.ts:146-148` | `segmentStartSeconds` and `segmentSeconds` on `SizeEstimateInput`, and the one-window fallback built from them | unreachable in the app: the one production caller always passes a non-empty `windows` (`src/lib/sizeEstimate.ts:287-295`) | keep: it is the input shape of `tests/fixtures/sizeEstimate.ts:6-11` and `stories/Savings.stories.ts:20-37` |
| `src/ui/compareTab.ts:334` | `?? encodeTest.startTime` for a sweep with no first window | live only on the unknown-length path; the value read is 0 unless a negative Duration follows an earlier run | keep |
| `src/lib/types.ts:234`, `src/lib/state.ts:58`, `src/ui/encodeTab.ts:261`, `src/ui/compareTab.ts:712` | `encodeTest.startTime` and its two writes | live only on the unknown-length path, through the row above; its other three readers are fallbacks listed here | keep |
| `src/lib/types.ts:223`, `src/lib/state.ts:48`, `src/ui/compareTab.ts:334` | `matrix.segmentStart` | live only on the unknown-length path, through `src/ui/compareTab.ts:704`; its other two readers (`src/ui/compareTab.ts:712`, `src/ui/compareTab.ts:736`) feed nothing live | keep |
| `src/ui/compareTab.ts:709-715` | `syncSegmentToMatrix` | half of each: the duration write is live and intentional, the `startTime` write feeds only the fallbacks | keep |
| `src/ui/compareTab.ts:701-705` | `matrixWindows` returns one window built from `matrix.segmentStart` and `matrix.segmentLength` | live and intentional: the path for a file with no usable length, documented by `tests/unit/compareTab.test.ts:921-943` | keep |
| `src/lib/types.ts:224`, `src/lib/state.ts:49`, `src/ui/compareTab.ts:335` | `matrix.segmentLength` | live and intentional: `src/ui/compareTab.ts:713-714` puts the Duration field back when a square is chosen (`tests/unit/compareTab.test.ts:621-637`) | keep |
| `src/ui/encodeTab.ts:260`, `src/lib/types.ts:222`, `src/lib/types.ts:225`, `src/lib/types.ts:240-241`, `src/ui/compareTab.ts:664-665`, `src/ui/compareTab.ts:700`, `src/ui/compareTab.ts:707-708` | comments that still speak of a start field, of the original drawn from `startTime`, or of the list as the exception | comments only | reword |

- **Proposed change**: In three steps, most certain first. (1) DC-3: the `fallbackStart` parameter and the one
  argument passed for it. (2) The first two rows together: `currentSizeEstimate` hands `encodeTest.windows` to
  `estimateForWindows` as it is, and `segDuration` leaves the type, the initial state and `loadEncodedIntoAB`.
  With them go the test of the empty-list state (`tests/unit/sizeEstimate.test.ts:439-445`), the two
  assertions on the field (`tests/unit/abPanel.test.ts:241`, `tests/unit/abPanel.test.ts:742`) and the values
  tests set on it by hand (`tests/unit/sizeEstimate.test.ts:408`, `tests/unit/sizeEstimate.test.ts:422`,
  `tests/unit/analysisTab.test.ts:247`; see DC-9). (3) Reword the comments in the last row: the original side
  is drawn from each segment's own window (`src/ui/abPanel.ts:458`), the Compare tab builds only a Duration
  and a Segments field (`src/ui/segmentRun.ts:63-84`), and a one-stretch sweep fills `matrix.windows` as well.
  The rest is not part of this change. Making `totals` required and dropping the fallback at
  `src/ui/abPanel.ts:128` are open to the maintainers at the cost of test rewrites: the `load` helper at
  `tests/unit/abPanel.test.ts:82-84` (22 calls) and 7 direct calls use a call shape the app never does
  (NV-35). Removing `startTime` and `matrix.segmentStart` would need a literal 0 at
  `src/ui/compareTab.ts:334`, which is the same value for a file of unknown length, the documented case, but
  not after a negative number has been typed into Duration following an earlier run (the sampler returns
  nothing for it, `src/lib/sizeEstimate.ts:257`, and `startTime` may then be non-zero), so it would change
  behaviour there and is left alone, together with the fallback at `src/ui/abPanel.ts:85`.
- **Confidence it's safe**: **Medium**, for steps 2 and 3 as written. OBSERVED: `encodedSize` has two writers
  (`src/lib/state.ts:73`, `src/ui/abPanel.ts:103`) and `windows` has two (`src/lib/state.ts:65`,
  `src/ui/abPanel.ts:104`); the two in `loadEncodedIntoAB` are consecutive synchronous statements, and the
  list stored there has at least one entry, because the function throws on an empty `blobs`
  (`src/ui/abPanel.ts:62`), the Compare tab passes a mapping of `matrixWindows()`, which never returns an
  empty list (`src/ui/compareTab.ts:701-705`, `src/ui/compareTab.ts:725-731`), and the sample run returns
  before encoding when it has no window (`src/ui/encodeTab.ts:249-252`). `resetState` replaces both members in
  one synchronous call (`src/lib/state.ts:108`). Tried in order to break it: a load that sets the size but not
  the list, a reset landing between the two writes, and a list shorter than the blobs (only through the
  overlap noted at `src/ui/abPanel.ts:85`, which this change leaves in place). Step 3 is comments only. Medium
  rather than High because step 2 removes a member of the shared state type and deletes a test, and `tests/`
  is outside the type check (`configs/tsconfig.json:6-14`), so a test that still sets the removed field keeps
  passing unnoticed.
- **Verification**: `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint`, then
  `npx vitest run --config configs/vitest.config.ts tests/unit/abPanel.test.ts tests/unit/compareTab.test.ts tests/unit/encodeTab.test.ts tests/unit/sizeEstimate.test.ts tests/unit/analysisTab.test.ts tests/unit/segmentRun.test.ts`
  and `npm run test:integration`.

#### CON-3: `ffmpegEngine` keeps a free-function facade over `FfmpegWorker`

- **Location**: `src/lib/ffmpegEngine.ts:217-222` (the default worker and its docstring),
  `src/lib/ffmpegEngine.ts:236-253` (three forwarders), `src/lib/ffmpegEngine.ts:259-271` (their one caller),
  `src/ui/inBrowserEncode.ts:12`, `tests/unit/ffmpegEngine.test.ts:113-168`
- **Issue**: `ensureFfmpegInput`, `deleteFfmpegFile` and `runFfmpegArgs` each forward one call to
  `defaultFfmpegWorker`, are exported, and are called in `src` from one place, `runFfmpegEncode`
  (`src/lib/ffmpegEngine.ts:265-269`). The sample runs and the sweep call an `FfmpegWorker`'s methods directly
  (`src/ui/segmentRun.ts:304`: `const { data } = await worker.run(args, outputName);`). Two docstrings still
  describe the earlier arrangement: `runFfmpegArgs` is said to be split out
  `for runs that encode one input many times`, naming the matrix sweep (`src/lib/ffmpegEngine.ts:247-248`),
  which called it only from commit 28bc126 (#21) to d951965 (#22); and the free functions are said to be kept
  because most of the `app has only ever needed the one core.` (`src/lib/ffmpegEngine.ts:219-220`), while one
  module imports any of them (`src/ui/inBrowserEncode.ts:12`). The cost is three exported names that read as a
  second way to drive the engine, and tests that reach the worker through them.
- **Proposed change**: Inline the three into `runFfmpegEncode` as calls on `defaultFfmpegWorker`
  (`ensureInput`, `run`, `deleteFile`), keeping its `try`/`finally`; delete the three exports, and with
  `runFfmpegArgs` its docstring; rewrite the docstring of `defaultFfmpegWorker`
  (`src/lib/ffmpegEngine.ts:217-221`) for what is true now (one caller, the whole-file encode). In the same
  commit move the three tests at `tests/unit/ffmpegEngine.test.ts:132-162` onto the worker's methods; one of
  them, the second write after a replaced core (`tests/unit/ffmpegEngine.test.ts:141-148`), is also asserted
  on a pooled core (`tests/unit/ffmpegEngine.test.ts:283-297`) and can be dropped instead. DUP-8 lists a pair
  of tests in this file that repeat each other through the two entry points: settle both in one pass.
  `resetFfmpeg` (`src/lib/ffmpegEngine.ts:224-226`) is another forwarder, the only one with no production
  caller: see DC-5. The remaining two, `setFfmpegHandlers` and `ensureFfmpegLoaded`
  (`src/lib/ffmpegEngine.ts:228-234`), stay, because the whole-file encode imports them.
- **Confidence it's safe**: **High**. OBSERVED: each forwarder is one awaited call on the same object, so the
  inlined function makes the same three calls in the same order. `git grep` for the three names over `src`,
  `tests` and `stories` returns only the module itself and `tests/unit/ffmpegEngine.test.ts`, and
  `tests/unit/inBrowserEncode.test.ts:18` mocks the module with the three functions
  `src/ui/inBrowserEncode.ts:12` imports, none of which is removed. If this were wrong, the failure would be a
  test still importing a removed name, which `tsc` does not see (`tests/` is outside the type check) and
  vitest does.
- **Verification**: `npm run typecheck`, then
  `npx vitest run --config configs/vitest.config.ts tests/unit/ffmpegEngine.test.ts tests/unit/inBrowserEncode.test.ts`.

#### CON-4: Field builders return only the wrapper, so callers find their controls again by id or class

- **Issue**: The form builders create a control, put it inside a wrapper and return the wrapper alone, so the
  code that wants the control looks it up again: by tag with a non-null assertion, by document id on every
  change, or by class. Each lookup brings a null path that cannot be taken with the builders the app uses, and
  five unit tests exist to walk those paths (three with bare elements, two on a page without the controls).
  `checkField` (`src/ui/encodeTab.ts:42`) and `progressBar` (`src/ui/formControls.ts:99`) already return their
  parts, which is the shape proposed for the rest. Established by reading every call of each builder in `src`,
  `tests` and `stories` and searching the tree for the ids and classes involved.

<!-- prettier-ignore -->
| Location | What is there | What it costs | Change | Safe? |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `src/ui/formControls.ts:18-62` | `fieldSelect` and `fieldNumber` return only the wrapping `div` | nine sites find the control again by tag and assert it exists (`src/ui/segmentRun.ts:69-70`; `src/ui/encodeTab.ts:91`, `src/ui/encodeTab.ts:150`, `src/ui/encodeTab.ts:155`, `src/ui/encodeTab.ts:160`, `src/ui/encodeTab.ts:170`, `src/ui/encodeTab.ts:185`, `src/ui/encodeTab.ts:191`) | return the field with its `select` or `input`; update the 11 calls in `src` and the calls in two test files | High |
| `src/ui/cliControls.ts:85-98`, `src/ui/cliControls.ts:112-138` | the command builder's controls are looked up by document id: two lookups when binding, six on every sync | six null guards and eight casts for one tab; three lines at build time repeat what the sync sets (`src/ui/encodeTab.ts:82`, `src/ui/encodeTab.ts:92`, `src/ui/cliControls.ts:79`) | sync through references the builders hand back, and call the sync once at the end of `renderEncodeTab` | Medium |
| `src/ui/formControls.ts:108-123`, `src/ui/formControls.ts:145-152`, `src/ui/formControls.ts:159-160`, `src/ui/formControls.ts:168-169` | `resetProgressFill` finds the fill again by class and returns it nullable; `clearLog` and `logLine` find the fold and its count by class | null arms that only tests reach, and an `if (fill)` in three callers (`src/ui/compareTab.ts:525`, `src/ui/encodeTab.ts:265`, `src/ui/inBrowserEncode.ts:48`) | carry the fill (optionally the fold) on `RunUi` and `EngineBox`, typed non-null; drop the three guards | Medium |
| `src/ui/segmentRun.ts:56-95` | `sampleFields` takes an `idPrefix` and marks its two inputs with classes, and `syncSampleFields` queries the document for "every copy" | there is one copy (`src/ui/compareTab.ts:92`); the prefix, the classes and the query serve a second that never shipped | correct the two docstrings now; optionally sync the two inputs the builder already holds | High (docstrings), Medium (the rest) |
| `src/ui/encodeTab.ts:41-52` | `checkField`, private to the tab, with the label's leading space supplied by each caller (`src/ui/encodeTab.ts:108-112`) | the one field builder that returns its control lives in a tab module; `src/ui/compareTab.ts:261-269` builds a checkbox in a label by hand | optional: move it to `src/ui/formControls.ts` and let it own the space | High |

- **Proposed change**: One direction for all five rows: a builder returns its field together with its control.
  (1) `fieldSelect` returns the field with its `select`, `fieldNumber` the field with its `input`, and the
  nine lookups become destructuring. (2) With the controls in hand, `resolutionFields` returns its two
  controls as well, `bindResolutionControls` and `syncQualityControls` take references instead of ids, and one
  call of the sync at the end of `renderEncodeTab` replaces the three build-time lines. The two
  `parentElement` guards at `src/ui/cliControls.ts:118` and `src/ui/cliControls.ts:128` go with it (DC-4). The
  tests of a page without the controls (`tests/unit/cliControls.test.ts:179-181`,
  `tests/unit/cliControls.test.ts:253-264`) and the build-time display check
  (`tests/unit/cliControls.test.ts:95`) are rewritten. (3) `runControls` and `engineBox` keep the `fill` that
  `progressBar()` already returns (both discard it today, `src/ui/segmentRun.ts:104`,
  `src/ui/formControls.ts:84`), the fill's type loses its null, and the three tests on bare elements
  (`tests/unit/formControls.test.ts:92-99`, `tests/unit/formControls.test.ts:101-112`,
  `tests/unit/formControls.test.ts:157-163`) are deleted or rewritten. (4) The docstring at
  `src/ui/segmentRun.ts:59-61` says both tabs edit the same numbers and the one at
  `src/ui/segmentRun.ts:86-87` speaks of every copy of the fields; since 93231de (#23), the commit that added
  `sampleFields`, the Reencode tab has had its own picker. The test comment at
  `tests/unit/segmentRun.test.ts:197-198` repeats the premise. Dropping `idPrefix`, the marker
  classes and the document query is optional and must keep three things: typing in one field still rewrites
  the other from the state, the call without an argument at `src/ui/compareTab.ts:714` still works, and the
  ids `etDuration` and `etSegments` stay. (5) `checkField` cannot serve the sweep's tick lists as it is: those
  are a `label.axis-option` with no surrounding field and a `value` in place of an `id`. What the two share is
  smaller, a label holding a checkbox and a text node that starts with a space, and only a helper of that size
  could be common to both. `@brain-bbqs/ui` 0.3.0 exports no checkbox builder. Moving `checkField` as it
  stands, with each label's text node left exactly as it is, is low value and the maintainers' call.
- **Confidence it's safe**: **High** for rows 1 and 5 and the docstrings of row 4, **Medium** for rows 2 and 3
  and the optional part of row 4. OBSERVED, row 1: the DOM built is the same; a search of `src` for a
  `querySelector` call followed by a non-null assertion returns exactly the nine lines listed, and no story
  calls either builder. Row 2: the six ids and `cmdPre` occur only in `src/ui/cliControls.ts`,
  `src/ui/encodeTab.ts` and their two test files, and the values the builders set at build time equal what the
  sync writes (compared line by line), so the page is unchanged; it is Medium because three tests are
  rewritten and two of them describe the tolerance as intended. Row 3: every bar and console in the app comes
  from `progressBar()` and `logConsole()` (`src/ui/segmentRun.ts:104-106`, `src/ui/formControls.ts:84-89`), so
  the same elements receive the same writes; no coverage threshold is configured
  (`configs/vitest.config.ts:4-9` passes none, and the shared Vitest config applies one only when given), so
  removing the three tests cannot fail the test step, but the comment at
  `tests/unit/formControls.test.ts:155-156` says not throwing is the behaviour, hence Medium. Row 4:
  `tests/unit/segmentRun.test.ts:185-186` and `tests/unit/segmentRun.test.ts:199-241` pin the classes and the
  two-copy case and would change, as would the two checks at `tests/unit/encodeTab.test.ts:81-82` if CL-6
  has moved them onto those classes. Row 5: a pure move; the tests find the builder's boxes by id
  (`tests/unit/encodeTab.test.ts:161-165`) and the tick lists by field label
  (`tests/unit/compareTab.test.ts:136-141`). `tests/` is outside the type check, so a missed test call fails
  when it runs, not in `tsc`.
- **Verification**: `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint`, then
  `npx vitest run --config configs/vitest.config.ts tests/unit/formControls.test.ts tests/unit/cliControls.test.ts tests/unit/encodeTab.test.ts tests/unit/segmentRun.test.ts tests/unit/compareTab.test.ts tests/unit/inBrowserEncode.test.ts`.

#### CON-5: Values threaded through signatures or patched in after construction

- **Issue**: Four places where a value reaches its user by a longer route than it needs: handed down through
  functions that never read it, written onto an object after the object is built, defined in a module that
  does not use it, or typed wider than any caller makes it. None is a defect today. The cost is in the next
  change: nine signatures that carry one argument to its single reader, two control objects that behave
  differently, six imports held for one function, and eight fallbacks for a state the callers have already
  excluded. Established by listing the callers of each function with `git grep` over `src`, `tests` and
  `stories` and following each chain to its reader.

<!-- prettier-ignore -->
| Location | What is there | What it costs | Change | Safe? |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `src/ui/compareTab.ts:291`, `src/ui/compareTab.ts:319`, `src/ui/compareTab.ts:450`, `src/ui/compareTab.ts:491`, `src/ui/compareTab.ts:640`, `src/ui/compareTab.ts:739`, `src/ui/compareTab.ts:789`; `src/ui/encodeTab.ts:213`, `src/ui/encodeTab.ts:242` | `vt`, the video track, is a parameter of nine functions that only pass it on; its one reader is `renderAbResult` (`src/ui/abPanel.ts:305`), reached through `loadEncodedIntoAB` | 25 lines of `src/ui/compareTab.ts` mention `vt` and two read it (`src/ui/compareTab.ts:87-88`); `resetStaleMatrix` takes the pair as `(ui, vt)`, the other six as `(vt, ui)` | carry it on `MatrixUi`, built once per render at `src/ui/compareTab.ts:101`, and read it at `src/ui/compareTab.ts:667` | High |
| `src/ui/abPanel.ts:316-317`, `src/ui/abPanel.ts:336-337`, `src/ui/abPanel.ts:362-363`, `src/ui/abPanel.ts:367` | the panel reads only width, height and rate off the track, with eight `?? 0` for an unknown size | both callers return before they get this far unless the size is known (`src/ui/compareTab.ts:88`, `src/ui/encodeTab.ts:57`), so the eight fallbacks run only in `tests/unit/abPanel.test.ts:713-728` | give the panel a width, a height and the packet rate, or narrow the parameter's type (which leaves the calls in `tests/unit/abPanel.test.ts` as they are); keep the rate fallback at `src/ui/abPanel.ts:497` | Medium |
| `src/ui/compareTab.ts:101-104`, `src/ui/segmentRun.ts:113-117` | `runControls` builds `syncRunAction` over a label frozen at render; the Compare tab spreads the result into a new object and overwrites the method on the copy | two `ui` objects that answer the same call differently: the Stop listener keeps the original (`src/ui/segmentRun.ts:117`) | `runControls` takes a function that returns the label; delete `src/ui/compareTab.ts:102-104` | High |
| `src/ui/cliControls.ts:140-158` | `showReencodeResult` sits in the command builder's module; its one caller is the whole-file encode (`src/ui/inBrowserEncode.ts:73`) | it is the sole user of six of that module's imports (`fmtBytes`, `h`, `gridItem`, `fmtSizeChangePct`, `fmtChangeFactor`, `EngineBox`) | move it into `src/ui/inBrowserEncode.ts`, and its two tests (`tests/unit/cliControls.test.ts:267-292`) into that module's test file | High |

- **Proposed change**: Rows 1 and 2 meet in the Compare tab. Put the track on `MatrixUi` and read it from
  there at `src/ui/compareTab.ts:667`: seven signatures lose a parameter and the argument-order mismatch goes
  with them. With row 2 as well, what goes on `MatrixUi` is the sized value the panel takes (the three
  numbers, or the track under its narrowed type), built once after the size check at
  `src/ui/compareTab.ts:88`. The Reencode tab's single hop (`src/ui/encodeTab.ts:213`,
  `src/ui/encodeTab.ts:242`) can stay. In the panel the eight `?? 0` (two, if row 4 of DUP-5 has been applied) and the unknown-size half of the
  test at `tests/unit/abPanel.test.ts:713-728` go; by a hand count `renderAbResult` sits at the configured complexity
  cap of 20 (`configs/eslint.config.js:9`) and these are eight of the twenty. The rate fallback stays: a real
  video track has a null `packetRate` whenever its packet statistics cannot be read
  (`src/lib/mediabunnyMeta.ts:12`, `src/lib/mediabunnyMeta.ts:19`), a case the loader is written and tested
  for (`tests/unit/mediabunnyMeta.test.ts:122-144`). Passing a `VideoInfo` instead is not proposed: its rate
  is `state.fps` (`src/ui/fileLoading.ts:111`), which differs from the track's own when the rate is missing
  (the panel paces at 30 frames a second today; `state.fps` falls back to samples over duration) and for a
  file with several video tracks (`src/lib/state.ts:113` takes the first, `src/lib/mediabunnyMeta.ts:68` keeps
  the last), so that swap would change behaviour. Row 3: `runControls` takes a function that returns the label
  instead of a string, and `syncRunAction` writes that function's result; the Compare tab passes
  `runActionLabel`, the Reencode tab a function returning its fixed label, and
  `tests/unit/segmentRun.test.ts:123` and `tests/unit/segmentRun.test.ts:246` change in the same commit. Row 4
  is a move of one function and two tests.
- **Confidence it's safe**: **High** for rows 1, 3 and 4, **Medium** for row 2. OBSERVED, row 1: every
  `(vt, ui)` pair starts in the same `renderCompareTab` call (`src/ui/compareTab.ts:87`,
  `src/ui/compareTab.ts:101`), so a field on `ui` holds the same captured object; the seven functions are
  private to the module and no test calls them. A live read of `videoTrackInfo()` at the point of use would
  not be equivalent: `tests/unit/compareTab.test.ts:1115-1137` empties the tracks in the middle of a run and
  expects the run to finish. Row 2: the eight lines are every read of the track in `src/ui/abPanel.ts`;
  `codedWidth` and `codedHeight` are written once, when the track is described
  (`src/lib/mediabunnyMeta.ts:30-31`), so a size checked at render is still there when the panel draws. It is
  Medium because half of a test is removed and because the narrowed type was not compiled. Row 3: the same
  strings are written at the same moments, since the first label is still computed inside the `runControls`
  call; the function has four callers (`src/ui/compareTab.ts:94`, `src/ui/encodeTab.ts:217` and the two test
  lines above) and `syncRunAction` four call sites (`src/ui/segmentRun.ts:508`, `src/ui/compareTab.ts:238`,
  `src/ui/compareTab.ts:300`, `tests/unit/segmentRun.test.ts:261`). In the Compare tab the original object's
  method is never called today: the Stop listener that holds it runs `requestStop`, which touches only the
  Stop button and the note (`src/ui/segmentRun.ts:482-488`), and both objects share those. Row 4: no test
  mocks `cliControls`, and `tests/unit/inBrowserEncode.test.ts:18-20` mocks only the engine, `@ffmpeg/util`
  and the save module, none of which the function touches; `src/ui/inBrowserEncode.ts:16` is that module's
  only import from `cliControls`, so the move creates no import cycle. `tests/` is outside the type check, so
  a missed test edit shows in vitest, not in `tsc`.
- **Verification**: `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint`, then
  `npx vitest run --config configs/vitest.config.ts tests/unit/compareTab.test.ts tests/unit/encodeTab.test.ts tests/unit/abPanel.test.ts tests/unit/segmentRun.test.ts tests/unit/cliControls.test.ts tests/unit/inBrowserEncode.test.ts`.

#### CON-6: `AtomRect` has two discriminants for one fact

- **Location**: `src/lib/atomLayout.ts:37-59` (the type); `src/lib/atomLayout.ts:188-201` and
  `src/lib/atomLayout.ts:234-246` (its two producers); `src/ui/atomsTab.ts:175`, `src/ui/atomsTab.ts:258`,
  `src/ui/atomsTab.ts:302` (consumers that test `kind`); `src/ui/atomsTab.ts:215`, `src/ui/atomsTab.ts:219`
  (consumers that test `box`)
- **Issue**: Two members state one fact: `kind: "box" | "group";` and, further down the same interface,
  `box: BoxNode | null;` under the docstring `The atom itself on a "box" rect; null on a "group".` The two
  producers always set them consistently, and consumers test whichever is to hand: three read `kind`, two read
  `box` (`return rect.box !== null ? rect.box.type : boxCount(rect.count);`). The type also admits the two
  combinations nothing produces, a box rect without an atom and a group with one, so a new producer or
  consumer can disagree with the others and still compile. Low priority.
- **Proposed change**: Tie the two in the type: a union of a box arm that carries `box: BoxNode` and a group
  arm that carries `box: null`, over the shared members. Keep `box: null` on the group arm. The variant that
  drops the member from the group arm is not type-only: `rect.box !== null` is true for a missing member, so
  groups would take the box branch at run time unless `src/ui/atomsTab.ts:215` and `src/ui/atomsTab.ts:219`
  move to `kind` in the same change.
- **Confidence it's safe**: **High** as proposed (the variant that keeps `box: null`); the variant that drops
  the member, which is not proposed, would be **Medium**. OBSERVED: the first is a change of declaration only, erased at build, and neither producer, no consumer
  and no test changes. Nothing outside `src/lib/atomLayout.ts` constructs an `AtomRect` (`git grep` over
  `src`, `tests` and `stories`), and no test compares a whole rect: they read single members, `r.box?.type`
  among them (`tests/unit/atomLayout.test.ts:38`), which is why the second variant is Medium. INFERRED, not
  compiled here: that both existing checks still narrow on the union.
- **Verification**: `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint`, then
  `npx vitest run --config configs/vitest.config.ts tests/unit/atomLayout.test.ts tests/unit/atomsTab.test.ts tests/unit/inspectTab.test.ts`.

#### CON-7: Three knowledge bases, three shapes

- **Location**: `src/lib/codecKb.ts:48-170` and `src/lib/explainers.ts:440-455` (two codec tables, joined at
  `src/lib/codecKb.ts:53,69,84,99,115,121,127,138,144,150,156,162,168`); `src/lib/explainers.ts:229-234`,
  `src/lib/explainers.ts:239-382`, `src/lib/metadataTagKb.ts:13-28` and `src/lib/metadataTagKb.ts:45-55` (six
  origins and six seed lists, zipped by hand)
- **Issue**: Two of the app's three knowledge bases join their copy to their lookup by hand. Codecs: two
  tables hold the same thirteen keys in the same order and are joined by thirteen lines of the form
  `description: CODEC_DESCRIPTIONS.avc,`. The description table is typed `Record<string, string>`
  (`src/lib/explainers.ts:440`) and the shared base tsconfig does not turn on `noUncheckedIndexedAccess`, so a
  mistyped key on one of those lines compiles and the codec card prints the word "undefined"
  (`src/lib/explainers.ts:433`). Tags: six origin strings and six seed lists are exported one by one and
  zipped pair by pair (`...seedGroup(QUICKTIME_ORIGIN, QUICKTIME_TAGS),` and five more); each of the twelve
  names occurs exactly three times in `src`, `tests` and `stories` (definition, import, that one use).
  Containers have the third shape, one table of whole records with a lookup beside it
  (`src/lib/explainers.ts:200-202`, `src/lib/containerKb.ts:8-11`).
- **Proposed change**: (a) Codecs, one of two and not both: remove `description` from the table's entries and
  let `describeCodec` read the text by the id it already holds, which deletes the thirteen lines; or keep the
  lines and give `CODEC_DESCRIPTIONS` a closed key type, so that a mistyped key stops compiling. (b) Tags:
  export one ordered list of groups, each an origin with its seeds, and build `TAG_KB` from it in the same
  order, since the case-insensitive fallback takes the first match (`src/lib/metadataTagKb.ts:45-47`,
  `src/lib/metadataTagKb.ts:62`). Leave `describeContainer` where it is: copy in `src/lib/explainers.ts`,
  lookups in the `*Kb` modules, is the stated design (`src/lib/explainers.ts:7-8`).
- **Confidence it's safe**: **Medium**. OBSERVED: the two codec tables' key lists are identical, so a join by
  id gives the same string for all thirteen. Of the 90 tag seeds none repeats a key exactly, and the only keys
  that collide once upper-cased are nine Vorbis entries against nine normalized ones, so that one order is all
  anything can observe; `tests/unit/metadataTagKb.test.ts:47-50` pins it. Medium because the values that move
  are unprotected today: no unit or integration test asserts a codec description (`tests/unit/codecKb.test.ts`
  checks families and details only; see NV-35), and three of the six origins (ID3, Vorbis, RIFF) are asserted
  nowhere. INFERRED: Chromatic's snapshot of the loaded Inspect tab
  (`tests/chromatic/app.chromatic.test.ts:19-24`) shows the fixture's H.264 and AAC cards and no other codec;
  and a join by id differs from today only for an id that is an inherited property name such as `constructor`,
  which is not a codec id mediabunny reports.
- **Verification**: First add the missing assertions: the thirteen descriptions through `describeCodec`, and
  the origins of `TIT2`, `ENCODER` and `ISFT`. Then `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint`,
  `npx vitest run --config configs/vitest.config.ts tests/unit/codecKb.test.ts tests/unit/metadataTagKb.test.ts tests/unit/containerKb.test.ts tests/unit/mediabunnyMeta.test.ts tests/unit/explainers.test.ts tests/unit/inspectTab.test.ts`
  and `npm run build`.

#### CON-8: Strings and numbers that the TypeScript, the stylesheets and `index.html` keep equal by hand

- **Issue**: Eleven values are written in more than one place and kept equal only by whoever edits them: an
  anchor and the heading it is slugified from, a pixel offset in a comparison and in a string, sizes that sit
  in TypeScript and in one or both stylesheets, sums worked out by hand inside `src/style.css`, and ids
  spelled in the page, in the lookups and once more in a module. No test compares one copy with another, apart
  from the page's ids, which `tests/unit/elements.test.ts:17` holds against the lookups. Two of the pairs can
  be reduced to one copy without changing anything; for most of the rest the tie is real (CSS cannot read a
  TypeScript constant) and the most that helps is a test that reads both sides. Every cited line was read and
  the sums were recomputed.

<!-- prettier-ignore -->
| Value | Where it is written | What breaks if one moves | Change | Safe? |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| the atom card's anchor, `#mp4-box-atom-structure` | typed out at `src/ui/inspectTab.ts:108`; the id itself is slugified from the heading at `src/ui/atomsTab.ts:80` (`src/ui/inspectToc.ts:32-36`) | reword the heading and the overview's link points nowhere; no test pins the href | export the card's title once and pass its slug (the document's own link at `src/ui/analysisTab.ts:119` is built that way today, but DC-1 deletes that line) | High |
| the scroll-spy trigger line, 96 | `src/ui/inspectToc.ts:61` (a comparison) and `src/ui/inspectToc.ts:65` (inside the `rootMargin` string) | the observer fires at one line and the handler picks the section by another | name the number once and build the string from it | High |
| the histogram's 90px height and 2px floor | `src/ui/seekTab.ts:142`; `src/style.css:1043`, `src/style.css:1050`; `src/lib/analysisDoc.ts:427`, `src/lib/analysisDoc.ts:430` | bars overflow or fall short of their box, on the page or in the document | keep: the bar heights are set in pixels and pinned at `tests/unit/seekTab.test.ts:434`, so no preserving edit removes the tie | no change |
| the document's content width, 772 | `src/ui/analysisTab.ts:88` restates 860 less twice 44 from `src/lib/analysisDoc.ts:222-237` | the atom map's labels are fitted to a width the document does not have | keep: with border-box and the 1px border the content box is 770, and changing the number could flip label fits in the exported file | no change |
| the atom label budget | `src/ui/atomsTab.ts:55-58` against the font size and padding in both sheets (`src/style.css:957`, `src/style.css:990`, `src/style.css:998-999`; `src/lib/analysisDoc.ts:396`, `src/lib/analysisDoc.ts:405`, `src/lib/analysisDoc.ts:407`) | a label is drawn where it no longer fits, or hidden where it would | a test that reads both sides, with a tolerance | Medium |
| the chart width, 600 | `src/ui/bitrateChart.ts:13`, `src/ui/seekTab.ts:212` against `max-width: 600px` at `src/style.css:1066`, `src/style.css:1107`, `src/lib/analysisDoc.ts:440` | INFERRED: at full width the drawing stops being one unit to a pixel, so its text scales | the same test, or nothing | Medium |
| nine theme keys | `src/lib/demoArchive.ts:64-78` against the nine `[data-hue]` rules at `src/style.css:2278-2304` | a renamed or new theme silently takes the fallback hue | the same test | Medium |
| the notch clamp, 20 | `src/ui/demosPage.ts:129` against the 9px triangle (`src/style.css:2364`) and the card's 8px radius (`src/style.css:519`) | the point can sit on the rounded corner | none; slight | no change |
| sums inside the stylesheet | `src/style.css:2257-2258` (158px a tile plus 24px, from `src/style.css:2455`, `src/style.css:2446`, `src/style.css:2261` and the 1px border); `src/style.css:1232` (258px from the 260px at `src/style.css:1226`) | a tile, gap or padding changes and the sum is stale | optional: custom properties for the parts, keeping `min-width: 176px` as a literal | Medium |
| two shell ids | `src/ui/elements.ts:22-23`, which are also the defaults of `getShellElements` in `@brain-bbqs/ui`; the page has them at `index.html:35` and `index.html:202` | nothing today; the two would not follow the package if its defaults moved, as the What's New ids do (`src/ui/elements.ts:26`) | optional: take the two elements from `getShellElements` | Medium |
| the error slot's id, `errorMsg` | `src/ui/elements.ts:7` and `index.html:87`, used by `src/ui/fileLoading.ts:35-42`; again at `src/ui/seekTab.ts:120`, behind a null guard | rename the id and a failed seeking test reports nothing | optional: one owner, with `src/main.ts` handing `renderSeekTab` the element or a callback | Medium |

- **Proposed change**: Do the first two rows: only there can one copy go with nothing else changing. For the
  anchor, the slug of "MP4 Box / Atom Structure" is the string typed today, so the link is unchanged. For the
  label budget, the chart width, the histogram and the theme keys, add one unit test that reads
  `src/style.css` and the document's stylesheet as text and compares them with the TypeScript constants, the
  way `tests/unit/elements.test.ts` reads `index.html`. The label costs need a tolerance: the paddings are
  exact (twice 5px, twice 2px) but the per-character figures are a rounded-up 0.6em and the docstring calls
  them rough (`src/ui/atomsTab.ts:51`). That the charts' 600 is meant to equal the CSS width is this review's
  inference; no comment says so. The last three rows are optional and low priority. In the stylesheet, 158n +
  24 checks out for the tile, gap, padding and border it is built from, but `min-width: 176px` does not follow
  that formula (one tile needs 182px), so a `calc()` for it would move it by 6px: keep it a literal. For the
  shell ids no line is saved and the ids stay spelled in `index.html` and in the tests; leaving them is
  defensible. For the error slot the id must stay registered in `getElements()`, because
  `tests/unit/elements.test.ts:17` fails on a page id that no lookup registers, and the change breaks the
  one-argument signature every renderer shares. It also leaves alone what a user can see today, that a later
  successful run never hides an earlier failure message: that part is NV-14.
- **Confidence it's safe**: **High** for the first two rows, **Medium** for the added test and for the three
  optional rows. OBSERVED, anchor: `slugify` lower-cases and collapses each run of other characters to one
  hyphen (`src/lib/analysisDoc.ts:19-26`), and the table of contents adds a suffix only to a repeated slug
  (`src/ui/inspectToc.ts:32-35`); the headings before this one, the overview and the video track, slug to
  other strings. No test pins the href, so a mistake here would not fail anything: follow the link once by
  hand. Trigger line: the same number and the same string, and `tests/unit/inspectToc.test.ts` asserts nothing
  about the observer's options. The added test: it changes nothing a user sees, but it was not written for
  this review, and of the TypeScript values it would compare only `DEMO_GROUPS` is exported today
  (`src/lib/demoArchive.ts:64`); the rest are unexported constants or literals inside a function, so reaching
  them is part of the work, hence Medium. Shell ids: the same `required()` and the same message for one
  missing id (`tests/unit/elements.test.ts:29-33`); the differences are one more lookup, of an
  `oauthSigninBtn` this page does not have, and, if the call is hoisted, which id is named first when several
  are missing. The shared contract helper fails only on a failed lookup, an unregistered id or a repeated id,
  so an absent optional id passes. Error slot: the app writes the same text with the same `display`;
  `src/main.ts:23` already throws at boot without the slot, so the guard at `src/ui/seekTab.ts:121` can fail
  only in `tests/unit/seekTab.test.ts:259-269`, and that test and the three failure tests
  (`tests/unit/seekTab.test.ts:221-257`) would be rewritten; `tests/integration/smoke.spec.ts:167` still finds
  the text in `#errorMsg`. INFERRED: what the Chromatic job would show. Its three page states
  (`tests/chromatic/app.chromatic.test.ts:5-24`) include the Demos page and the Inspect tab of a loaded file,
  and the fixture's own comment says its bitrate plot is on screen in the snapshots
  (`tests/fixtures/demoVideo.ts:57-58`), so tile sums, hues, atom labels, the bitrate chart and the histogram
  are in a snapshot; the seek scatter and the sample preview are in none.
- **Verification**: `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint`, `npm test` and `npm run build`; for the optional rows
  also `npm run test:integration`, and `npm run test:chromatic` together with the Chromatic job's diff of the
  "Demos page" and "Main page - demo file loaded" snapshots.

#### CON-9: The command builder's defaults are mirrored and hand-reset in tests, and the mirror has drifted

- **Location**: `src/lib/state.ts:82-96` (the defaults); `tests/fixtures/state.ts:19-33` (`BASE_CLI`, the
  mirror); `tests/fixtures/state.ts:35-46` (`resetCliDefaults`); `tests/unit/cliControls.test.ts:59`;
  `tests/unit/encodeTab.test.ts:170-180`; `tests/unit/compareTab.test.ts:688-689`
- **Issue**: The builder's defaults exist in six places: the source literal, a hand-written mirror and four
  hand resets. The mirror has drifted: `BASE_CLI` lacks `customScale` (`src/lib/state.ts:95`), a required
  field of the `CliState` it is annotated with (`src/lib/types.ts:131`), which nothing reports because tests
  are not type-checked (NV-30). `resetCliDefaults` restores five of the thirteen fields
  (`tests/fixtures/state.ts:40-44`), so three test files put others back themselves: seven in
  `tests/unit/encodeTab.test.ts:173-179`, `customScale` in `tests/unit/cliControls.test.ts:59`, two in
  `tests/unit/compareTab.test.ts:688-689`. The first two came with #45, two days after #39 created the
  fixture; the compareTab pair dates from #23 and was left in place by #39. It runs after its test's
  assertions, so a failing assertion would leak `audioMode` and `faststart` into the tests after it.
- **Proposed change**: Give the defaults one home and send every reset through it. Tests-only form: complete
  `BASE_CLI` (add `customScale: 60`, or make it a copy of `cli` taken when the fixture module loads, which
  removes the mirror), have `resetCliDefaults` assign all of `BASE_CLI` onto `cli` in place of its five
  assignments, delete the three per-file resets, and reword `tests/fixtures/state.ts:35-39`. Alternative: a
  factory for the defaults exported from `src/lib/state.ts`, from which `cli`, the fixture and the reset are
  built. It works and leaves the app's behaviour alone, with one caveat: it adds an export only tests use
  and, as a change under `src/`, needs a version bump. The tests-only form is sufficient.
- **Confidence it's safe**: **High**. OBSERVED: every writer of `cli` was listed. In `src` they are event
  handlers (`src/ui/cliControls.ts:88`, `src/ui/cliControls.ts:94-95`, `src/ui/encodeTab.ts:152-194`); in
  tests, the resets above, the writes they undo (`tests/unit/compareTab.test.ts:680-681`, `customScale` in
  the cliControls tests, the fields edited through the page inside `tests/unit/encodeTab.test.ts:169-309`)
  and direct writes to the five fields the fixture already restores; `gopOverride` has no writer. The fear
  that a full reset would expose tests relying on a leaked value was checked and does not hold: in the
  seven files that call `resetCliDefaults` every test already starts with all thirteen fields at their
  defaults. `customScale` is read only in `src/ui/cliControls.ts:73,88,94-95`, and the two users of
  `BASE_CLI` (`tests/unit/cliCommand.test.ts:17`, `tests/unit/qualityMatrix.test.ts:89-116`) assert on
  named fields and built arguments, never on the whole object. INFERRED: the copy-at-load variant relies on
  vitest giving each test file its own module registry.
- **Verification**: `npm test` and `npm run lint` (the nine files that use `resetCliDefaults` or `BASE_CLI`
  are the ones to watch); for the factory form also `npm run typecheck` and `npm run build`.

#### CON-10: Four ways a unit test captures a download

- **Location**: `tests/unit/save.test.ts:78-100`; `tests/unit/seekTab.test.ts:284-338`;
  `tests/unit/analysisTab.test.ts:14-22` with `tests/unit/analysisTab.test.ts:89-99`;
  `tests/unit/inBrowserEncode.test.ts:15-24`
- **Issue**: Four test files capture a download in four ways. The save test exercises `downloadBlob` itself
  behind stubbed static methods of `URL` and a spied anchor click; the seekTab test runs the real one behind its own
  stubs of the same things and reads the Blob back asynchronously; the analysisTab test mocks it and
  subclasses `Blob` so the text can be read synchronously; the inBrowserEncode test mocks it with a bare
  `vi.fn`. One loose end is concrete: the save test replaces `URL.createObjectURL` and
  `URL.revokeObjectURL` with `Object.defineProperty` (`tests/unit/save.test.ts:83-84`) and never puts them
  back (its only cleanup is `tests/unit/save.test.ts:44-47`), and turns fake timers off on line 99 outside
  any `finally`, where the seekTab test restores both descriptors (`tests/unit/seekTab.test.ts:284-293`)
  and wraps its timers in one (`tests/unit/seekTab.test.ts:320-328`). The seekTab revoke assertion
  (`tests/unit/seekTab.test.ts:348`) also re-tests what `tests/unit/save.test.ts:98` covers.
- **Proposed change**: In `tests/unit/save.test.ts`, save and restore the two descriptors the way the
  seekTab test does, and put the fake-timer section in a `finally`. One shared helper does not fit the
  four: one that reads a Blob would have a single user, since only the seekTab test reads a Blob
  asynchronously and the analysisTab capture is synchronous by design (`tests/unit/analysisTab.test.ts:78-83`
  returns the text of a click that is not awaited). Going further is a maintainers' call: mocking
  `src/lib/save` in the seekTab test, as the other two tab tests do, would remove about 45 lines of stubs
  (`tests/unit/seekTab.test.ts:284-328`) and the duplicate assertion, but its two export tests would no
  longer show the export reaching a real download link.
- **Confidence it's safe**: **Medium** as a whole; the cleanup alone is High. OBSERVED: only
  `src/lib/save.ts:33` and `src/lib/save.ts:40` read the two static methods, and the test that replaces them is
  the last in its file. INFERRED: that the leftovers cannot reach another file rests on vitest isolating
  test files by default (neither `configs/vitest.config.ts` nor the shared config sets `isolate` or a pool
  option). The optional step is the Medium part: `src/lib/save.ts` stays covered by its own test, but what
  the two seekTab tests demonstrate would narrow.
- **Verification**: `npx vitest run --config configs/vitest.config.ts tests/unit/save.test.ts`, the same for
  `tests/unit/seekTab.test.ts`, and `npm test` if the optional step is taken.

### Cleanup

#### CL-1: Comments and docstrings in `src/lib`, `src/ui` and `src/main.ts` that do not describe the code

- **Issue**: Each row is a comment or docstring that the code beside it contradicts (the last two are a
  displaced paragraph and a grammar slip). The project keeps its reasoning in comments by rule
  (`CLAUDE.md:8`), so a wrong comment is a wrong record of why: it sends a reader to look for a reuse switch, a
  start field, a second engine, a click handler on the drop zone, or a loop that is in another file. Most rows
  were left by six pull requests: #22 (d951965, the resolution axis and the worker pool), #23 (93231de, six
  tabs folded into three), #25 (d3a7c25, the Educational switch and the Inspect contents list), #28 (ee118b0,
  the demos page), #29 (36fc51b, several stretches in the A/B window) and #35 (fa82244, the tooltip and
  explainer rework). Since names the pull request that left the comment wrong; "never true (#N)" means #N
  wrote the comment and its merged commit already contradicted it, which holds for 24 of the 50 rows in whole
  or in part. Each quote was read at its line and dated with `git log -S` on the quoted phrase.

  nothing compiled, drawn, exported or stored can differ (INFERRED from the kind of edit; nothing was run).
  OBSERVED: each quote was read at the cited line, and the code that contradicts it at the lines in the third
  column. For the rows marked never true, the merged commit that introduced the phrase was read as well: for
  example d3a7c25 already has four renderer calls above "three renderers", the drop zone has only drag
  listeners in the original single-file page, in #3, #23 and #28 and at this commit, and `sampleFields` had one

  all: a reuse control, a start field, a mode, a stored tab, a reader of `path`, a Bitrate item on the Video
  Track card, and a loop in `src/ui/compareTab.ts` that takes work off the queue (the one loop over it below
  the comment, `src/ui/compareTab.ts:578-580`, only marks the squares never reached). None exists. The
  optional move of the GOP block is the one edit that touches code: the four constants reference no other
  value, and an export does not depend on where in the module it is declared (INFERRED). What can go wrong is
  formatting: `npm run format:check` may object to the layout of a reworded comment, and the pre-commit
  codespell hook reads comment text. A pull request that touches `src/` also needs the version bump the
  Version Check job enforces (`.github/workflows/version-check.yml:7-11`).

#### CL-2: Comments in `src/style.css` that describe something other than the rule under them

- **Issue**: Sixteen comments in the stylesheet are tabled here because they do not describe the rule they sit on
  (DC-7 rewords one more, `src/style.css:1823-1824`, with the rule it deletes). Eleven state something
  the rule, the markup or the TypeScript contradicts: a 220px rail over a 210px rule, a fold per demo file on a
  page that has none, a media query "at the end of this file" that is above the comment. Five are displaced:
  each sits above a different rule from the one it describes, or stacked on the comment that does describe the
  rule under it. The file is 2508 lines long and its comments are how a reader finds a rule, so each one costs
  a wrong turn. Every comment was read against the rule under it and against the markup or TypeScript it
  names, and dated with `git blame` and `git log -S` (Since reads as in CL-1).

  wrench, and at ee118b0 the watermark query was already above the comment that points to the end of the

  under it and against the code it names. Since reads as in CL-1.

  in 53 files, as CI's last run on this commit did, with one test renamed. With the optional assertion, run
  `npx vitest run --config configs/vitest.config.ts tests/unit/inspectTab.test.ts` first. The two
  integration rows change comments only; `npm run test:integration` covers them if wanted.

#### CL-4: Names left over from removed designs

- **Issue**: Six of the rows below carry the vocabulary of designs the app has left behind: the fast and
  exact in-browser encoders (one engine since #23), the Seek and Atoms tabs (sections of Inspect since #23,
  so that `src/main.ts:34` now reads `renderSeekTab(els.panels.inspect);`) and the "Encode Test" tab label
  (changed in #12). The last two rows are names that say something other than what the code holds. Nothing
  behaves wrongly; the cost is a maintainer looking for the fast counterpart of `runExactEncode`, for a Seek
  tab or for an encode test, and finding none. Each name was searched across the whole tree and traced in
  the history.

<!-- prettier-ignore -->
| Location | What is there | Why the name does not fit | Change | Safe? |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `src/ui/inBrowserEncode.ts:25,38` | `runExactEncode`: private, two references | Its counterpart `runFastEncode` went with the WebCodecs engine in #23 (93231de), so "exact" contrasts with nothing | Rename | High |
| `src/ui/formControls.ts:64,78` | `EngineBox` and its builder `engineBox` | Before #23 the builder's first argument chose the fast or the exact engine; one box is left. Defensible while the module calls ffmpeg.wasm the engine (`src/ui/inBrowserEncode.ts:3`) | Optional rename | High |
| `src/ui/formControls.ts:75-76`, `src/ui/tabs.ts:17-18` | Comments: `so there is one explainer rather than two stacked`; `the in-browser encoders` | #35 (fa82244) removed the explainer above the box in the one section that uses the builder; there has been one encoder since #23 | Reword | High |
| `src/ui/seekTab.ts:25` | `renderSeekTab`: draws the "GOP / Keyframe Structure" section into the Inspect panel | The Seek tab became a section of Inspect in #23 (`src/lib/appUrl.ts:27`) | Rename, 14 lines | High |
| `src/ui/seekTab.ts`, `src/ui/atomsTab.ts` | File names; the other four `*Tab.ts` modules each match a `data-tab` in `index.html:128-140` | Named for the Seek and Atoms tabs retired in #23 (`src/lib/appUrl.ts:26-27`) | Optional file moves | Medium |
| `src/lib/state.ts:56,98`, `src/lib/types.ts:233` | `encodeTest`, `EncodeTestState`, `initialEncodeTest` | "Encode Test" was the Compare tab's label until #12 (447fc41); the object also holds the Reencode tab's run (`src/lib/types.ts:235-237`), so it names neither tab | Rename; `encodeTest` alone occurs 332 times | Medium |
| `src/lib/format.ts:1,18` | `fmtRate(hz)` under a header that lists "sample rates" | All five callers pass frames per second; the audio sample rate is formatted inline (`src/ui/inspectTab.ts:221`) | Parameter to `fps`; drop "sample rates" | High |
| `src/lib/codecKb.ts:34,131` | `AAC_OTI`: module-private, two references | Its index is the N of `mp4a.40.N` (`src/lib/codecKb.ts:129`), the audio object type; the object type indication is the 40 | Rename, for example `AAC_OBJECT_TYPES` | High |

- **Proposed change**: The first, third, seventh and eighth rows are local and can go with any nearby edit.
  `renderSeekTab` is named on 14 lines: `src/main.ts:20,34`, `src/ui/seekTab.ts:25`, `src/ui/inspectToc.ts:12`
  (a comment), `tests/unit/inspectTab.test.ts:8,118,250`, `tests/unit/main.loaded.test.ts:44,53,82` (a mock
  factory entry and two labels) and `tests/unit/seekTab.test.ts:41,66,266,440`. `encodeTest` alone occurs 115
  times in 8 files under `src` and 217 times in 8 files under `tests`; the type is also named at
  `src/lib/state.ts:11,40` and the initialiser at `src/lib/state.ts:108`. Give the three a commit of their
  own. The two optional renames reach further. `EngineBox` is also named at `src/ui/cliControls.ts:22,144`,
  `src/ui/inBrowserEncode.ts:17,38` and `tests/unit/cliControls.test.ts:14,268,273`; `engineBox` at
  `src/ui/inBrowserEncode.ts:17,23` and `tests/unit/formControls.test.ts:4,166,168`; the header comment at
  `src/ui/formControls.ts:1` follows it. Moving the two files touches their importers (`src/main.ts:11,20`,
  `src/ui/analysisTab.ts:74,78`, `stories/AtomMap.stories.ts:3`), the path strings in four test files, two
  comments (`src/ui/inspectTab.ts:2-3`, `src/style.css:1062`) and the two test file names. Keep the
  `?tab=seek` and `?tab=atoms` aliases (`src/lib/appUrl.ts:25-29`). Left to NV-39, because a user or a test
  can see them: the `et` prefix of the sample fields' ids and of `etUpscaleView`
  (`src/ui/compareTab.ts:92`, `src/ui/abPanel.ts:408`), the in-core file names `et_in`, `et_snip_` and
  `et_out_` (`src/ui/segmentRun.ts:131,171,284`), the labels that mix "segment" with "sampled stretch", and
  `is_sync` (`src/lib/types.ts:63`). A single word for window, segment, stretch and snippet is not proposed:
  they are not synonyms. `SampleWindow` is a time range (`src/lib/types.ts:185`), `AbSegment` pairs one with
  the decoder of its encoded copy (`src/lib/types.ts:197`), and a snippet is the cut file in the encoder's
  in-memory filesystem (`src/ui/segmentRun.ts:171`).
- **Confidence it's safe**: **High** for six rows, **Medium** for the two wide ones (last column). OBSERVED: a
  whole-tree `git grep -n -w` for each name returns only the lines listed or counted here, all in `src`,
  `tests` and one story; the engine names were also searched case-insensitively and hyphenated. No DOM id,
  class, CSS selector, user-visible string, storage key, URL parameter or workflow carries any of them, and
  no test mocks `src/lib/state`, so every use of `encodeTest` is a named import. The two Medium rows are
  wide, not doubtful: two thirds of the `encodeTest` occurrences and six of the path strings are in tests,
  which only a test run checks. If a rename misses a value import or a mock entry, the test fails when it
  runs (INFERRED for `tests/unit/main.loaded.test.ts:76-86`). One miss is silent:
  `tests/unit/cliControls.test.ts:14` imports `type EngineBox`, the test runner erases type-only imports, and
  `tests/` is outside the TypeScript project (`configs/tsconfig.json:6-14`), so a stale type name there passes
  typecheck, lint and every test (INFERRED). The story's import is compiled only by a Storybook build (the
  `build-storybook` script, `package.json:20`), which none of the commands below runs. Hence the grep.
- **Verification**: `npm run typecheck && npm run lint && npm test && npm run build`, then
  `git grep -n -w -E "runExactEncode|EngineBox|engineBox|renderSeekTab|encodeTest|EncodeTestState|initialEncodeTest|AAC_OTI"`
  for whichever names were renamed, and `git grep -n -E "seekTab|atomsTab"` after a file move: each must
  print nothing.

#### CL-5: Local idioms

- **Issue**: Twenty places take a longer or less direct route than the idiom used a few lines away or
  elsewhere in the same module. None is a defect and none changes what the app does; each makes a maintainer
  stop to work out whether the difference is meant. Every row was confirmed by reading the code around it
  and its callers. Matters of taste are left out, import order among them (no lint or formatter rule orders
  imports here).

| --- | --- | --- | --- |
